Potential algorithm details:

Fast convolution:
```
def next_pow2(N): return 1 << (N-1).bit_length()
def convolve_real_fast(a,b):
    N = a.size + b.size -1
    nfft = 1 << (N-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)
    return c[:N]
```

```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-x.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0: room=np.where((x<1)[0], 1-x, 0); s=room.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; x += (diff/s)*room
       else: floor=x; s=x.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; x += (diff/s)*x
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    # exact adjust by residual over free variables
    res=target-x.sum()
    if res !=0:
       if res>0: free = (x<1-1e-15)&(x>0? maybe any x<1); add distribute among free until 1, leftovers to x=0? For exact.
       else similar.
    return x
```

Since f(τ)=∑clip(z_i-τ,0,1) monotone decreasing. τ in [min z -1, max z]. Bisection 50 iterations O(m). Simpler and accurate. For target in [0,m]. Use if target<=0 or >=m. Bisection:
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    target = float(target)
    lo = float(x.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(x.max())
    # f(lo)=sum max(x_i-lo,0)? with clip to 1. Need ensure.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0,1))
       if s>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    return np.clip(x-tau,0,1)
```

Grad:
```
def ls_grad(x):
   c, full = compute_full_c(x)
   weights = softmax(c, alpha)
   # compute gradient via FFT correlation
   y = 1.0 - 2.0*full
   yrev = y[::-1]
   nfft_g = next_pow2((2n-1)+n-1) = 4n-2 maybe
   W=np.fft.rfft(weights, nfft_g)
   Y=np.fft.rfft(yrev, nfft_g)
   gfull = np.fft.irfft(W*Y, nfft_g)[n-1:n-1+n] * dx
   gv = gfull[:m] + gfull[m:][::-1]
   gv += 2*beta*(x.sum()-target)
   return gv
```

    if len(src) == n: full=src
    else: x_src=np.linspace(0,2,len(src)); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n); full=np.interp(x_new,x_src,src)

```
def lse_obj(x, n, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target):
   m=n//2; full=make_full(x,n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(full,nfft); B=onesfft - A
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:2*n-1]*(2.0/n)
   # if c negative tiny? leave
   mval=np.max(c)
   sh=alpha*(c-mval)
   shmax=np.max(sh)
   s=np.exp(sh-shmax).sum()
   val = mval + (shmax+np.log(s))/alpha + beta*(x.sum()-target)**2
   return float(val)
```
Need if alpha small and c vary; fine.

```
def lse_grad(x, n, alpha, beta, nfft_c, onesfft, nfft_g, target):
   compute c, weights
   # weights vector length 2n-1
   y = 1.0 - 2.0*full
   yrev = y[::-1]
   W=np.fft.rfft(weights, nfft_g); Y=np.fft.irfft? use rfft
   g = np.fft.irfft(W*Y, nfft_g)
   gfull = g[n-1:n-1+n]*(2.0/n)
   gv = gfull[:m] + gfull[m:][::-1]
   gv += 2.0*beta*(x.sum()-target)
   return gv
```

def select_active(c, max_active=32, rel_tol=0.005, abs_tol=1e-8):
   L=len(c); mval=c.max()
   thr=mval - max(abs_tol, rel_tol*mval)
   idx=np.where(c>=thr)[0]
   if len(idx)>max_active: score=c[idx]; idx[idx.argsort(-score)[:max_active]]
   # sym include mirror for each idx (L-1-j), dedup
   set...
   return np.array(sorted)
```

`project_box_sum(x,target)` bisection details:
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    target=float(target)
    if target <= 0: return np.zeros_like(x)
    if target >= x.size: return np.ones_like(x)
    x0=x.clip(0,1)? For bisection, input can be outside? We can clip first.
    z=np.clip(x,0,1)
    lo=float(z.min()) - 1.0
    hi=float(z.max())
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(z-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    out=np.clip(z-tau,0.0,1.0)
    # adjust residual
    diff=target-out.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # distribute over free
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(1-out)[::-1]? maybe choose free with room
          for idx in np.argsort(-(1-out)): ...
       else: order=np.argsort(out)[::-1] ...
    return out
```
Bisection 80*O(m) for m=2048 ~163k ops per projection; many projections maybe okay but can be slower. We can reduce to 50 iterations. Use vectorized. Alternative iterative distribute fast. Bisection 50*2048=100k ~0.0005s. okay.
Adjust residual: after bisection sum within 1e-12 usually. To be safe, do few free adjustments.
```
res = target - out.sum()
if res > 0:
   free = np.where(out < 1.0-1e-15)[0]
   # distribute equally among free with room
   if len(free)>0:
      add = min(res/len(free), (1-out[free]).min())
      out[free]+=add; res-=add*len(free)
      if res>1e-15:
         for idx in np.argsort((1-out), generic): ...
```
Could complicate. Bisection enough.

But project_box_sum with z clipped assumes x within or outside. If x from L-BFGS within bounds due L-BFGS-B, okay. If not, clip. Good.

Now, `make_full_from_half(v)`:
```
def make_full_from_half(v):
    h=np.empty(2*v.size,dtype=float)
    h[:v.size]=v
    h[v.size:]=v[::-1]
    return h
```
Target full sum = 2v.sum. Good.

If using initial full with odd length, resample to even n. Parent length 500 even but not multiple 4; we choose n sequence multiples 4. `resample_half(parent,n)`:
```
src=np.asarray(parent)
if src.size != n:
    x_src=np.linspace(0,2,src.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n)
    full=np.interp(x_new,x_src,src)
else: full=src.copy()
return project_box_sum(half_from_full(full,n), n/4.0)
```

def pair_local_search(v, n, deadline, nfft, onesfft, top=20):
    v=project_box_sum(v,n/4)
    best_c=quick_c(v,...)[1]
    best_v=v.copy()
    ds=[0.05,0.02,0.005,0.001,0.0002,5e-5]
    for sweep in range(20):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       c, full = compute_c_full(v,...)
       alpha=100000.0; beta=0.0
       # compute gradient without beta via same lse_grad, returns gv
       gv = lse_grad(v,n,alpha,0.0,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g, n/4) # target only for penalty zero? beta=0 -> no constant
       neg=np.argsort(gv)[:top]; pos=np.argsort(-gv)[:top]
       improved=False
       for ii in neg:
          i=int(ii)
          for jj in pos:
             j=int(jj)
             if i==j: continue
             dmax = min(1.0 - v[i], v[j])
             if dmax <= 1e-12: continue
             for d in ds:
                if d < dmax:
                   v2=v.copy(); v2[i]+=d; v2[j]-=d
                   c2=quick_c(v2,...)[1]
                   if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                      best_c=c2; best_v=v2; improved=True
                      v=v2.copy(); break
                elif d == dmax? use <=.
          if time.time()>deadline: break
       if not improved: break
    return project_box_sum(best_v,n/4), best_c
```

`quick_c` top-level:
```
def quick_c(v,n,nfft,onesfft):
   if len(v)==n//2: full=make_full(v)
   else: full=v
   A=np.fft.rfft(full,nfft); B=onesfft-A
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:2*n-1]*(2.0/n)
   return float(c.max())
```

Detailed functions:
```
def full_from_vars(x, sym, n):
    if sym:
        h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
        m = n // 2
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = x[::-1]
        return h
    return x

def target_for(n, sym): return 0.25*n if sym else 0.5*n

def grad_from_full(g, x, sym, n):
    if sym:
       m=n//2
       return g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]
    return g

def quick_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft):
   h=full_from_vars(...)
   A=...
   c=...
   return float(np.max(c))
```

```

`lse_grad(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target)`:
```
h=full_from_vars; c; weights; yrev=(1-2*h)[::-1]; W=rfft(weights,nfft_g); Y=rfft(yrev,nfft_g); g=irfft(W*Y,nfft_g)[n-1:n-1+n]*(2/n); gv=grad_from_full(g,x,sym,n); gv += 2*beta*(x.sum()-target); return gv
```
Since derivative wrt variable x: if variable maps to multiple full positions, sum. Good.

```
def default_vars(n,sym,seed,variant):
   target=target_for(n,sym)
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   if variant==0: h=0.5*np.ones(n)
   elif variant==1: h=0.5+0.45*np.cos(np.pi*(x-1.0))
   elif variant==2: h=0.5+0.45*np.sin(np.pi*x)
   elif variant==3:
      f=0.5+0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)+0.2*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
      h=0.5+0.5*(f-f.min())/(f.max()-f.min())? maybe.
   else:
      # low freq random
      h=0.5*np.ones(n)
      for freq in [1,2,3]:
         amp=0.15/rng? rng.uniform(0.1,0.3)
         ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
         h += amp*np.sin(2π*freq*x/ph?)
   if sym:
      h=0.5*(h+h[::-1]); v=h[:n//2]
   else: v=h.copy()
   return project_box_sum(v,target)
```
Ensure h within [0,1] before project (clip). variant 3 maybe use threshold: h = (0.5+0.45*cos(...)+0.2*sin(...)). Clip.

Implement direct helper:
```
def direct_lag_gradient_weighted(weights, h, n):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for j in range(len(weights)):
       w=weights[j]
       if w>1e-14:
          i0=max(0,j-(n-1)); i1=min(n-1,j)
          if i1<i0: continue
          q0=j-i1; q1=j-i0
          g[i0:i1+1] += w*(1.0-2.0*h[q0:q1+1])
    return g*(2.0/n)
```
This function top-level. It uses Python loop over length L (8191) even if few weights; better use flatnonzero. But `np.flatnonzero` over 8191 cheap. For weights dense, use FFT.

if len(idx) <= 128: loop idx.
```

```
for _ in range(5):
   diff=target-out.sum()
   if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
   if diff > 0:
      room=1.0-out
      order=np.argsort(-room)
      for idx in order:
         idx=int(idx); add=min(diff, room[idx])
         if add>0:
            out[idx]+=add; diff-=add
         if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   else:
      order=np.argsort(out)[::-1]
      for idx in order:
         sub=min(-diff, out[idx])
         if sub>0: out[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
         if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
```

Now, exact eval `compute_c5_exact(h)`:
```
def compute_c5_exact(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(c.max())*dx
```
Maybe `np.correlate` for int? h float. Good.

Now, `resample_full(src,n)`:
```
def resample_full(src,n):
    src=np.asarray(src,dtype=float)
    if src.size==n: return src.copy()
    if src.size==0: return 0.5*np.ones(n)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,src.size)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    out=np.interp(xn,xs,np.clip(src,0,1))
    return out
```
No endpoint issue. For step function, linear interp okay.

`half_from_full(full,n)`:
```
def half_from_full(full,n):
    m=n//2
    v=0.5*(full[:m] + full[m:][::-1])
    return project_box_sum(v,n/4.0)
```
If n not even? We only use even. If n odd, use first? Not.

Now, default starts. Let's write robust:
```
def default_vars(n,sym,seed,variant):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed + variant*101)
    xfull=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
    if variant % 4 == 0:
       h=np.full(n,0.5)
    elif variant % 4 == 1:
       h=0.5 + 0.45*np.cos(np.pi*(xfull-1.0))
    elif variant % 4 == 2:
       h=0.5 + 0.45*np.sin(np.pi*xfull)
    else:
       h=0.5*np.ones(n)
       for f in (1,2,3):
          amp=rng.uniform(0.10,0.30)
          ph=rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi)
          h += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*xfull + ph)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if sym:
       h=0.5*(h + h[::-1])
       v=h[:n//2].copy()
       target=n/4.0
    else:
       v=h.copy(); target=n/2.0
    return project_box_sum(v,target)
```
Variant 2 sin zero at endpoints, high at 1, maybe good. Variant1 high at edges and low center? cosπ(x-1): at 0 cos -π=-1, h=0.05; at1 cos0=0.95, at2 -1; high center. Good. Actually cos π(x-1) at x=0: cos(-π)=-1, low; x=1 high. Good.

Detailed function implementations:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_PHASE_DEADLINE = None

def time_callback(*args):
    if _PHASE_DEADLINE is not None and time.time() > _PHASE_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("time limit")

def next_pow2(n):
    if n <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(n) - 1).bit_length()
```
Note int(n)-1 bit_length works.

`project_box_sum` as above. Ensure if x scalar? x array.

`make_full_from_vars(x, n, sym)`:
```
def make_full_from_vars(x,n,sym):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if sym:
       h=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
       m=n//2
       h[:m]=x
       h[m:]=x[::-1]
       return h
    else:
       return np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
```
Assumes len x correct.

`target_for(n,sym)`.

`full_from_vars` same as make.

`quick_c_vars(x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)`:
```
def quick_c_vars(x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft):
    h=make_full_from_vars(x,n,sym)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*(onesfft-A),nfft)
    c=c[:2*n-1]*(2.0/n)
    return float(np.max(c))
```
Potential negative tiny: max may be slightly less than exact. okay.

`compute_c_vars(x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)` returns c,h.

`direct_weighted_gradient(weights, h, n)` for direct in lse_grad:
```
def direct_weighted_gradient(weights, h, n):
    g=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    idx=np.flatnonzero(weights > 1e-13)
    for jj in idx:
       w=float(weights[jj]); j=int(jj)
       i0 = j - (n-1)
       if i0 < 0: i0=0
       i1 = j
       if i1 >= n: i1=n-1
       if i1 < i0: continue
       q_start = j - i0
       q_end = j - i1
       # q_start >= q_end
       g[i0:i1+1] += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[q_end:q_start+1][::-1])
    return g*(2.0/n)
```
If q_end==q_start, slice [q:q+1][::-1] okay. If q_end=0,q_start=0 -> [0:1][::-1]. Good.

`lse_obj`:
```
def lse_obj(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,target):
    h=make_full_from_vars(x,n,sym)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*(onesfft-A),nfft)[:2*n-1]*(2.0/n)
    mval=float(np.max(c))
    sh=alpha*(c-mval)
    shmax=float(np.max(sh))
    s=np.exp(sh-shmax).sum()
    if s <= 0: s=1.0
    val=mval + (shmax + float(np.log(s)))/alpha
    if beta != 0.0:
       val += beta*(float(np.sum(x))-target)*(float(np.sum(x))-target)
    return float(val)
```
Alpha >0. If alpha huge and c.mval, okay. If c has nan due h inf? no.

`lse_grad`:
```
def lse_grad(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target):
    h=make_full_from_vars(x,n,sym)
    A=np.fft.rfft(h,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*(onesfft-A),nfft)[:2*n-1]*(2.0/n)
    mval=float(np.max(c))
    sh=alpha*(c-mval)
    shmax=float(np.max(sh))
    w=np.exp(sh-shmax); s=w.sum(); w=w/s
    # if many zero use direct
    nz = np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-13)
    if nz <= 160:
       gfull=direct_weighted_gradient(w,h,n)
    else:
       yrev=(1.0-2.0*h)[::-1]
       W=np.fft.rfft(w, nfft_g)
       Y=np.fft.rfft(yrev, nfft_g)
       gfull=np.fft.irfft(W*Y, nfft_g)[n-1:n-1+n]*(2.0/n)
    gv=grad_from_full(gfull,x,sym,n)
    if beta != 0.0:
       gv = gv + (2.0*beta)*(float(np.sum(x))-target)
    return gv
```
Need `grad_from_full` top-level:
```
def grad_from_full(g,x,sym,n):
  if sym:
    m=n//2
    return g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]
  else:
    return g.copy()
```
Potential if x length n (full), return g. Good.

`active_grad`:
```
def active_grad(x,n,sym,active,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,target):
   h=make...
   c=compute...
   vals=c[active]
   if alpha>0:
      mval=vals.max(); sh=alpha*(vals-mval); shmax=...; w=np.exp(...); w/=w.sum()
   else:
      w=np.full(len(active),1.0/len(active))
   gfull=np.zeros(n)
   for idx,wj in zip(active,w):
      if wj > 1e-14:
         j=int(idx)
         i0=max(0,j-(n-1)); i1=min(n-1,j)
         if i1>=i0:
            q_start=j-i0; q_end=j-i1
            gfull[i0:i1+1] += wj*(1.0 - 2.0*h[q_end:q_start+1][::-1])
   gfull *= 2.0/n
   gv=grad_from_full(...)
   if beta: gv += 2*beta*(sum-target)
   return gv
```
Here active is numpy array. In `run_active_phase`, active fixed. Good.

`run_lse_phase` top-level:
```
def run_lse_phase(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,maxiter,deadline,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target,bounds):
    global _PHASE_DEADLINE
    _PHASE_DEADLINE = deadline
    m=len(x)
    maxcor=min(50, max(10, m//2))? Use 50.
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-20, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':20, 'maxfun': int(max(100, int(maxiter)*40)), 'maxcor':50, 'callback':time_callback}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_obj, x, jac=lse_grad, args=(n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,target), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
       y=res.x
    except Exception:
       y=x
    if y is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y=x
    y=project_box_sum(y,target)
    c=quick_c_vars(y,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
    return y,c
```
Need pass `nfft_g` to lse_grad args: `args=(n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target)`. `bounds` list. If `maxiter` 0 return x,c quickly.
`maxcor` if m<50? use min(50,m). If m=0 impossible. Use `min(50, max(5,m))`.

`run_active_phase` similar:
```
def run_active_phase(x,n,sym,active,alpha,beta,maxiter,deadline,nfft,onesfft,target,bounds):
    global _PHASE_DEADLINE
    _PHASE_DEADLINE=deadline
    try res=minimize(active_obj, x, jac=active_grad, args=(n,sym,tuple(active),alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,target), bounds=bounds, options=...)
    except: y=x
    y=project_box_sum(y,target); c=quick_c_vars(...); return y,c
```

`select_active(c,max_active)`:
```
def select_active(c,max_active):
    L=len(c); mval=float(np.max(c))
    tol=max(1e-9, 0.01*mval)
    idx=np.where(c >= mval - tol)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    # pair value
    pair_val=np.maximum(c, c[::-1])
    if len(idx) > max_active:
       # choose top indices by pair_val, but ensure include idx? Just choose all.
       top_order=np.argsort(-pair_val)
       selected_pair=top_order[:max(2,max_active//2)]
       idx_set=set()
       for j in selected_pair:
          idx_set.add(int(j)); idx_set.add(int(L-1-j))
       # If not enough, add top original idx
       for j in idx:
          if len(idx_set)>=max_active: break
          idx_set.add(int(j)); idx_set.add(int(L-1-j))
       if len(idx_set)>max_active:
           # keep highest pair_val
           arr=np.array(sorted(idx_set))
           vals=np.array([max(float(c[j]),float(c[L-1-j])) for j in arr])
           keep=np.argsort(-vals)[:max_active]
           idx_set=set(int(arr[i]) for i in keep)
       idx=np.array(sorted(idx_set))
    else:
       # include mirrors to keep symmetry
       idx_set=set(int(j) for j in idx); 
       for j in list(idx_set): idx_set.add(int(L-1-j))
       if len(idx_set)>max_active:
           # trim similarly
    return idx
```
Need list comprehension uses max? okay, not lambda. But code with list comp no closure. Fine.
Simplify:
```
def select_active(c, max_active):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    L=c.size
    mval=c.max()
    tol=max(1e-8, 0.005*mval)
    idx=np.where(c >= mval - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    # include mirrors
    setlist=[]
    for j in idx:
       setlist.append(int(j)); setlist.append(int(L-1-int(j)))
    # unique preserving top? use set
    uniq=list(set(setlist))
    if len(uniq) > max_active:
       pairv=np.array([max(float(c[j]), float(c[L-1-j])) for j in uniq])
       order=np.argsort(-pairv)[:max_active]
       uniq=[uniq[i] for i in order]
    return np.array(sorted(uniq), dtype=int)
```

`pair_local_search` top-level:

def pair_local_search(x,n,sym,deadline,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target, top=24, max_sweeps=20):
    x=project_box_sum(x,target)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=quick_c_vars(...)
    ds=(0.05,0.02,0.005,0.001,0.0002,5e-5,1e-5)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       c,h=compute_c_vars(best_x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
       act=select_active(c, 96)
       gv=active_grad(best_x,n,sym,act,0.0,0.0,nfft,onesfft,target)
       # directional derivative for pair inc i dec j = gv_i - gv_j
       neg=np.argsort(gv)[:top]
       pos=np.argsort(-gv)[:top]
       improved=False
       for ii in neg:
          i=int(ii)
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          for jj in pos:
             j=int(jj)
             if i==j: continue
             dmax=min(1.0 - best_x[i], best_x[j])
             if dmax <= 1e-14: continue
             for d in ds:
                if d <= dmax:
                   x2=best_x.copy(); x2[i]+=d; x2[j]-=d
                   # if near bounds due float, project? sum preserved; clip maybe
                   x2=np.clip(x2,0,1)
                   # projection could change sum slightly; maybe no need. But if clipping, sum changes. Use small d so no clip. If after clip sum off, project_box_sum.
                   x2=project_box_sum(x2,target)
                   c2=quick_c_vars(x2,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
                   if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                      best_c=c2; best_x=x2; improved=True; break
             if improved:
                # recompute? continue outer? Maybe restart pair loops after update? We can break both to avoid using stale too much.
                break
          if improved: break
       if not improved: break
    best_x=project_box_sum(best_x,target)
    best_c=quick_c_vars(best_x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
    return best_x,best_c
```

def benchmark_vars(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target):
   t0=time.perf_counter()
   lse_obj(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,target)
   t1=time.perf_counter()
   lse_grad(x,n,sym,alpha,beta,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target)
   t2=time.perf_counter()
   obj_time=t1-t0; grad_time=t2-t1
   cost=grad_time + 6.0*obj_time + 0.001
   if cost < 1e-4: cost=1e-4
   return cost

def subgrad_refine(x,n,sym,deadline,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target):
    x=project_box_sum(x,target); best=x.copy(); best_c=quick_c...
    eta=0.05

       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c,h=compute_c_vars(best...)
       mval=c.max(); tol=max(1e-8,1e-4*mval)
       act=np.where(c>=mval-tol)[0]
       if act.size>96: act=act[np.argsort(-c[act])[:96]]
       gv=active_grad(best,n,sym,act,0.0,0.0,...)
       # feasible direction: remove mean? For projected gradient, use gv directly with projection. But to respect sum, project.
       improved=False
       for step_factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05]:
           d = eta*step_factor
           x2 = best - d*gv
           x2 = project_box_sum(x2,target)
           c2=quick_c_vars(x2,...)
           if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
              best=x2; best_c=c2; improved=True; eta=min(eta*1.5,0.2); break
       if not improved:
           eta*=0.6
           if eta<1e-6: break
    return best, best_c

Top-level `subgrad_refine`:
```
def subgrad_refine(x,n,sym,deadline,nfft,onesfft,target):
    x=project_box_sum(x,target)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_c=quick_c_vars(...)
    scale=1.0
    for _ in range(300):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        c,h=compute_c_vars(best_x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
        act=select_active(c,128)
        gv=active_grad(best_x,n,sym,act,0.0,0.0,nfft,onesfft,target)
        gv=gv - np.mean(gv)
        gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(gv))
        if gnorm < 1e-18: break
        d=gv/gnorm
        improved=False
        for k in range(8):
            s=0.1*scale*(0.5**k)
            if s < 1e-6: break
            x2=project_box_sum(best_x - s*d, target)
            c2=quick_c_vars(x2,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_x=x2; best_c=c2; improved=True; scale=min(2.0, scale*1.2); break
        if not improved:
            scale *= 0.6
            if scale < 1e-5: break
    best_x=project_box_sum(best_x,target)
    best_c=quick_c_vars(best_x,n,sym,nfft,onesfft)
    return best_x,best_c
```
This may move by large step 0.1; objective could worsen but accepts only improve. Good. It computes c and active_grad each iter (c FFT + direct active). 300* maybe 0.01=3s. okay.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    total=float(budget_s)
    reserve=15.0
    main_deadline=t0+total-reserve
    # initialize best
    ...
    main_budget=total-reserve
    # if parent and time for full512
    if parent is not None:
       frac0=0.08
       phase0_end=t0+main_budget*frac0
       if phase0_end > time.time()+25 and phase0_end < main_deadline:
           x0=resample_full(best_full,512); x0=project_box_sum(x0,256)
           x,c=optimize_vars(x0,512,False,phase0_end)
           full=...; ce=...; update
    # Prepare sym sequence fractions
    if parent is not None:
       seq=[(1024,0.16),(2048,0.40),(4096,0.28)]
    else:
       seq=[(1024,0.20),(2048,0.44),(4096,0.30)]
    cum=0.0
    for n, frac in seq:
       cum_end = min(t0 + main_budget*(cum + frac), main_deadline - 2)
       cum += frac
       if cum_end <= time.time() + 15: continue
       # choose start candidates
       candidates=[best_full]
       if parent is not None and parent is not best_full: candidates.append(parent)
       # choose sym start with exact only if n<=4096; do exact
       x0=None; x0c=inf
       for cand in candidates:
           fulltmp=resample_full(cand,n)
           v=half_from_full(fulltmp,n)
           cf=make_full_from_vars(v,n,True)
           cc=compute_c5_exact(cf)
           if cc<x0c: x0c=cc; x0=v
       if x0 is None: x0=default...
       # also if default no parent, maybe variant2
       x,c=optimize_vars(x0,n,True,cum_end)
       full=...; ce=...; update
    # optional 8192 if best_c>target and time left
    if best_c > 0.38085 and time.time() < main_deadline - 120:
       n=8192
       end=min(time.time()+120, main_deadline-2)
       candidates=[best_full]
       x0=choose sym start
       x,c=optimize_vars(x0,n,True,end)
       update
    # final subgrad/pair on best
    final_deadline=t0+total-5
    if best_vars is not None and time.time() < final_deadline - 20:
       target=target_for(best_n,best_sym)
       nfft=next_pow2(2*best_n-1); onesfft=...
       nfft_g=next_pow2(3*best_n-2)
       x,c=subgrad_refine(best_vars,best_n,best_sym,min(time.time()+max(10,(final_deadline-time.time())*0.4), final_deadline-15), nfft, onesfft, target)
       full=... exact update
    if best_vars is not None and time.time() < final_deadline - 5:
       target=...; nfft=...
       x,c=pair_local_search(best_vars,best_n,best_sym,final_deadline-1,nfft,onesfft,nfft_g,target)
       full=... exact update
    # final project and return exact
```

```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    z=np.clip(x,0,1)
    target=float(np.clip(target,0,z.size))
    for _ in range(30):
       diff=target-z.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-z; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          z += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          tot=z.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          z += (diff/tot)*z
       z=np.clip(z,0,1)
    # final residual
```

Pseudo:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    z=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    m=z.size
    target=float(target)
    if target <= 0: return np.zeros(m)
    if target >= m: return np.ones(m)
    z=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        s=z.sum()
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - z
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            # To avoid overshooting bounds, add proportionally; after scale, some may hit 1 if room small? We scale by diff/tot*room, max add for element = diff/tot <=1 if diff<=tot, so safe if diff<=tot. Since target<=m-s, diff<=m-s=tot? room sum=m-s, yes diff<=tot, so factor<=1, no overshoot.
            z += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot=s
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            z += (diff / tot) * z
        # clip tiny
        z=np.clip(z,0.0,1.0)
    # exact residual
    for _ in range(5):
        diff=target-z.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room=1.0-z
            order=np.argsort(-room)
            for idx in order:
                idx=int(idx); add=min(diff,room[idx]); 
                if add <=0: continue
                z[idx]+=add; diff-=add
                if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        else:
            order=np.argsort(z)[::-1]
            for idx in order:
                idx=int(idx); sub=min(-diff,z[idx])
                if sub <=0: continue
                z[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
                if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    return z
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_PHASE_DEADLINE = None


def time_callback(*args):
    if _PHASE_DEADLINE is not None and time.time() > _PHASE_DEADLINE:
        raise RuntimeError("phase time limit")


def next_pow2(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def target_for(n, sym):
    if sym:
        return 0.25 * float(n)
    return 0.5 * float(n)


def make_full_from_vars(x, n, sym):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if sym:
        h = np.empty(int(n), dtype=float)
        m = int(n) // 2
        h[:m] = x
        h[m:] = x[::-1]
        return h
    return x.copy()


def grad_from_full(g, x, sym, n):
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
    if sym:
        m = int(n) // 2
        return g[:m] + g[m:][::-1]
    return g.copy()


def project_box_sum(x, target):
    z = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    m = z.size
    target = float(target)
    if m == 0:
        return z
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    if target >= float(m):
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float)

    z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(35):
        s = float(z.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - z
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            z += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            z += (diff / tot) * z
        z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(6):
        diff = target - float(z.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - z
            order = np.argsort(-room)
            for idx in order:
                idx = int(idx)
                add = min(diff, room[idx])
                if add <= 0.0:
                    continue
                z[idx] += add
                diff -= add
                if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                    break
        else:
            order = np.argsort(z)[::-1]
            for idx in order:
                idx = int(idx)
                sub = min(-diff, z[idx])
                if sub <= 0.0:
                    continue
                z[idx] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                    break
    return z


def resample_full(src, n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if src.size == 0:
        return 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    if src.size == n:
        return np.clip(src, 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, src.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    return np.interp(xn, xs, np.clip(src, 0.0, 1.0))


def half_from_full(full, n):
    m = int(n) // 2
    v = 0.5 * (full[:m] + full[m:][::-1])
    return project_box_sum(v, 0.25 * float(n))


def compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft):
    h = make_full_from_vars(x, n, sym)
    a = np.fft.rfft(h, nfft)
    b = onesfft - a
    c = np.fft.irfft(a * b, nfft)
    c = c[:2 * int(n) - 1] * (2.0 / float(n))
    return c, h


def quick_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft):
    c, _ = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return float(np.max(c))


def direct_weighted_gradient(weights, h, n):
    n = int(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(weights > 1e-13)
    for jj in idx:
        w = float(weights[jj])
        j = int(jj)
        i0 = j - (n - 1)
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = j
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1
        if i1 < i0:
            continue
        q_start = j - i0
        q_end = j - i1
        g[i0:i1 + 1] += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[q_end:q_start + 1][::-1])
    return g * (2.0 / float(n))


def lse_obj(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target):
    c, _ = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    mval = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - mval)
    shmax = float(np.max(sh))
    s = float(np.exp(sh - shmax).sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    val = mval + (shmax + float(np.log(s))) / alpha
    if beta != 0.0:
        ssum = float(np.sum(x))
        val += beta * (ssum - target) * (ssum - target)
    return float(val)


def lse_grad(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target):
    c, h = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    mval = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - mval)
    shmax = float(np.max(sh))
    w = np.exp(sh - shmax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        w = w / s
    else:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[np.argmax(c)] = 1.0

    nz = int(np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-13))
    if nz <= 160:
        gfull = direct_weighted_gradient(w, h, n)
    else:
        yrev = (1.0 - 2.0 * h)[::-1]
        wfft = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft_g)
        yfft = np.fft.rfft(yrev, nfft_g)
        gfull = np.fft.irfft(wfft * yfft, nfft_g)
        gfull = gfull[int(n) - 1:int(n) - 1 + int(n)] * (2.0 / float(n))

    gv = grad_from_full(gfull, x, sym, n)
    if beta != 0.0:
        gv += (2.0 * beta) * (float(np.sum(x)) - target)
    return gv


def active_obj(x, n, sym, active, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target):
    c, _ = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    vals = c[active]
    if alpha > 0.0:
        mval = float(np.max(vals))
        sh = alpha * (vals - mval)
        shmax = float(np.max(sh))
        s = float(np.exp(sh - shmax).sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            s = 1.0
        val = mval + (shmax + float(np.log(s))) / alpha
    else:
        val = float(np.mean(vals))
    if beta != 0.0:
        ssum = float(np.sum(x))
        val += beta * (ssum - target) * (ssum - target)
    return float(val)


def active_grad(x, n, sym, active, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target):
    c, h = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    vals = c[active]
    if alpha > 0.0:
        mval = float(np.max(vals))
        sh = alpha * (vals - mval)
        shmax = float(np.max(sh))
        w = np.exp(sh - shmax)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            w = w / s
        else:
            w = np.zeros_like(vals)
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
    else:
        la = len(active)
        if la == 0:
            w = np.zeros(0)
        else:
            w = np.full(la, 1.0 / float(la))

    n = int(n)
    gfull = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx, wj in zip(active, w):
        wj = float(wj)
        if wj <= 1e-14:
            continue
        j = int(idx)
        i0 = j - (n - 1)
        if i0 < 0:
            i0 = 0
        i1 = j
        if i1 >= n:
            i1 = n - 1
        if i1 < i0:
            continue
        q_start = j - i0
        q_end = j - i1
        gfull[i0:i1 + 1] += wj * (1.0 - 2.0 * h[q_end:q_start + 1][::-1])

    gfull *= 2.0 / float(n)
    gv = grad_from_full(gfull, x, sym, n)
    if beta != 0.0:
        gv += (2.0 * beta) * (float(np.sum(x)) - target)
    return gv


def benchmark_vars(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target):
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    lse_obj(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target)
    t1 = time.perf_counter()
    lse_grad(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target)
    t2 = time.perf_counter()
    cost = (t2 - t1) + 9.0 * (t1 - t0) + 0.0015
    if cost < 1e-4:
        cost = 1e-4
    return float(cost)


def run_lse_phase(x, n, sym, alpha, beta, maxiter, deadline, nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target, bounds):
    global _PHASE_DEADLINE
    maxiter = int(maxiter)
    if maxiter <= 0:
        x = project_box_sum(x, target)
        return x, quick_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)

    _PHASE_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    m = len(x)
    options = {
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "ftol": 1e-20,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxfun": int(max(100, 35 * maxiter)),
        "maxls": 20,
        "maxcor": min(50, max(5, m)),
        "callback": time_callback,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_obj,
            x,
            jac=lse_grad,
            args=(n, sym, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        y = res.x
    except Exception:
        y = x

    if y is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(y, dtype=float))):
        y = x
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    y = project_box_sum(y, target)
    c = quick_c_vars(y, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return y, float(c)


def run_active_phase(x, n, sym, active, alpha, beta, maxiter, deadline, nfft, onesfft, target, bounds):
    global _PHASE_DEADLINE
    maxiter = int(maxiter)
    active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    if maxiter <= 0 or active.size == 0:
        x = project_box_sum(x, target)
        return x, quick_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)

    _PHASE_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    m = len(x)
    options = {
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "ftol": 1e-20,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxfun": int(max(100, 30 * maxiter)),
        "maxls": 20,
        "maxcor": min(50, max(5, m)),
        "callback": time_callback,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            active_obj,
            x,
            jac=active_grad,
            args=(n, sym, active, alpha, beta, nfft, onesfft, target),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        y = res.x
    except Exception:
        y = x

    if y is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(y, dtype=float))):
        y = x
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    y = project_box_sum(y, target)
    c = quick_c_vars(y, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return y, float(c)


def select_active(c, max_active):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    L = c.size
    mval = float(np.max(c))
    tol = max(1e-8, 0.005 * mval)
    idx = np.where(c >= mval - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

    tmp = []
    for j in idx:
        j = int(j)
        tmp.append(j)
        tmp.append(L - 1 - j)
    uniq = list(set(tmp))
    if len(uniq) > max_active:
        pairv = np.empty(len(uniq), dtype=float)
        for i, j in enumerate(uniq):
            a = float(c[j])
            b = float(c[L - 1 - j])
            pairv[i] = a if a >= b else b
        order = np.argsort(-pairv)[:max_active]
        uniq = [uniq[int(i)] for i in order]
    return np.array(sorted(uniq), dtype=int)


def subgrad_refine(x, n, sym, deadline, nfft, onesfft, target):
    x = project_box_sum(x, target)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    scale = 1.0

    for _ in range(320):
        if time.time() > float(deadline):
            break
        c, _ = compute_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
        act = select_active(c, 128)
        gv = active_grad(best_x, n, sym, act, 0.0, 0.0, nfft, onesfft, target)
        gv = gv - float(np.mean(gv))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gv))
        if gnorm < 1e-18:
            break
        d = gv / gnorm

        improved = False
        for k in range(9):
            step = 0.12 * scale * (0.5 ** k)
            if step < 1e-6:
                break
            x2 = project_box_sum(best_x - step * d, target)
            c2 = quick_c_vars(x2, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_x = x2
                best_c = float(c2)
                scale = min(2.5, scale * 1.15)
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            scale *= 0.62
            if scale < 1e-5:
                break

    best_x = project_box_sum(best_x, target)
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return best_x, float(best_c)


def pair_local_search(x, n, sym, deadline, nfft, onesfft, target):
    x = project_box_sum(x, target)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    if n <= 1024:
        top = 28
    elif n <= 2048:
        top = 24
    else:
        top = 16

    ds = (0.05, 0.02, 0.008, 0.003, 0.001, 0.0003, 0.00008)

    for _ in range(40):
        if time.time() > float(deadline):
            break
        c, _ = compute_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
        act = select_active(c, 96)
        gv = active_grad(best_x, n, sym, act, 0.0, 0.0, nfft, onesfft, target)

        neg = np.argsort(gv)[:top]
        pos = np.argsort(-gv)[:top]
        improved = False

        for ii in neg:
            i = int(ii)
            if time.time() > float(deadline):
                break
            for jj in pos:
                j = int(jj)
                if i == j:
                    continue
                dmax = min(1.0 - float(best_x[i]), float(best_x[j]))
                if dmax <= 1e-14:
                    continue
                for d in ds:
                    if d < dmax:
                        ni = float(best_x[i]) + d
                        nj = float(best_x[j]) - d
                        if ni <= 1.0 and nj >= 0.0:
                            x2 = best_x.copy()
                            x2[i] = ni
                            x2[j] = nj
                            c2 = quick_c_vars(x2, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
                            if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
                                best_x = x2
                                best_c = float(c2)
                                improved = True
                                break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    best_x = project_box_sum(best_x, target)
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return best_x, float(best_c)


def default_vars(n, sym, seed, variant):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) + 101 * int(variant))
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    if variant % 4 == 0:
        h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    elif variant % 4 == 1:
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.cos(np.pi * (x - 1.0))
    elif variant % 4 == 2:
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(np.pi * x)
    else:
        h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
        for f in (1, 2, 3):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            h += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if sym:
        h = 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])
        v = h[:n // 2].copy()
        return project_box_sum(v, 0.25 * float(n))
    return project_box_sum(h, 0.5 * float(n))


def optimize_vars(x0, n, sym, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    target = target_for(n, sym)
    x = project_box_sum(np.asarray(x0, dtype=float), target)

    nfft = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    ones = np.zeros(nfft, dtype=float)
    ones[:n] = 1.0
    onesfft = np.fft.rfft(ones)
    nfft_g = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * len(x)

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)

    remaining = float(deadline) - time.time()
    if remaining < 8.0:
        return best_x, float(best_c)

    est = benchmark_vars(x, n, sym, 100.0, 10.0 / float(n), nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target)
    t_start = time.time()
    total_budget = max(5.0, float(deadline) - t_start)

    smooth_phases = (
        (50.0, 0.75 / float(n), 0.045),
        (200.0, 2.0 / float(n), 0.055),
        (1000.0, 5.0 / float(n), 0.075),
        (5000.0, 15.0 / float(n), 0.095),
        (20000.0, 40.0 / float(n), 0.115),
        (100000.0, 100.0 / float(n), 0.135),
        (500000.0, 300.0 / float(n), 0.150),
    )

    for alpha, beta, frac in smooth_phases:
        rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
        phase_time = total_budget * float(frac)
        cap = rem - 1.0
        if phase_time > cap:
            phase_time = cap
        if phase_time < 8.0:
            continue
        maxit = int(phase_time / est * 0.42)
        if maxit < 25:
            continue
        if maxit > 6000:
            maxit = 6000
        phase_end = time.time() + phase_time
        x2, c2 = run_lse_phase(
            x, n, sym, alpha, beta, maxit, phase_end,
            nfft, onesfft, nfft_g, target, bounds
        )
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = float(c2)
            best_x = x2.copy()
        if c2 < best_c + max(1e-9, 1e-5 * max(1.0, best_c)):
            x = x2
        else:
            x = best_x

    rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
    if rem > 35.0:
        active_fracs = (0.50, 0.34, 0.24)
        active_alphas = (10000.0, 100000.0, 1000000.0)
        active_betas = (80.0 / float(n), 300.0 / float(n), 1200.0 / float(n))
        active_sizes = (80, 112, 144)
        for aa in range(3):
            rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
            if rem < 12.0:
                break
            phase_time = rem * active_fracs[aa] - 1.0
            if phase_time < 12.0:
                break
            ccur, _ = compute_c_vars(x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
            active = select_active(ccur, active_sizes[aa])
            if active.size < 2:
                break
            maxit = int(phase_time / est * 0.38)
            if maxit < 20:
                maxit = 20
            if maxit > 5000:
                maxit = 5000
            phase_end = time.time() + phase_time
            x2, c2 = run_active_phase(
                x, n, sym, active, active_alphas[aa], active_betas[aa], maxit, phase_end,
                nfft, onesfft, target, bounds
            )
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = float(c2)
                best_x = x2.copy()
            if c2 < best_c + max(1e-9, 1e-5 * max(1.0, best_c)):
                x = x2
            else:
                x = best_x

    rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
    if rem > 35.0:
        sub_end = min(float(deadline) - 1.0, time.time() + max(12.0, rem * 0.28))
        x2, c2 = subgrad_refine(x, n, sym, sub_end, nfft, onesfft, target)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = float(c2)
            best_x = x2

    rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
    if rem > 12.0:
        x2, c2 = pair_local_search(x, n, sym, float(deadline) - 0.5, nfft, onesfft, target)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = float(c2)
            best_x = x2

    best_x = project_box_sum(best_x, target)
    best_c = quick_c_vars(best_x, n, sym, nfft, onesfft)
    return best_x, float(best_c)


def compute_c5_exact(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * dx


def finalize_vars(x, n, sym):
    target = target_for(n, sym)
    x = project_box_sum(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), target)
    h = make_full_from_vars(x, n, sym)
    c = compute_c5_exact(h)
    return x, h, float(c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)
    reserve = min(15.0, 0.05 * total)
    main_budget = max(10.0, total - reserve)
    main_deadline = t0 + total - reserve
    final_deadline = t0 + total - 2.0

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if parent.size < 16:
            raise ValueError("parent too small")
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_vars = None
    best_full = None
    best_sym = False
    best_n = 0
    best_c = float("inf")
    warm_full = None

    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size % 2 != 0:
            parent = resample_full(parent, 512)
        parent = project_box_sum(parent, 0.5 * float(parent.size))
        xv, hv, cv = finalize_vars(parent, int(parent.size), False)
        best_vars = xv
        best_full = hv
        best_sym = False
        best_n = int(parent.size)
        best_c = cv
        warm_full = hv
    else:
        n = 1024
        xv = default_vars(n, True, seed, 1)
        xv, hv, cv = finalize_vars(xv, n, True)
        best_vars = xv
        best_full = hv
        best_sym = True
        best_n = n
        best_c = cv
        warm_full = hv

    if total >= 1000.0 and parent is not None:
        specs = (
            (512, False, 0.08),
            (1024, True, 0.14),
            (2048, True, 0.40),
            (4096, True, 0.28),
            (8192, True, 0.10),
        )
    elif total >= 700.0 and parent is not None:
        specs = (
            (512, False, 0.12),
            (1024, True, 0.18),
            (2048, True, 0.46),
            (4096, True, 0.24),
        )
    elif total >= 300.0 and parent is not None:
        specs = (
            (512, False, 0.16),
            (1024, True, 0.36),
            (2048, True, 0.48),
        )
    elif total >= 150.0 and parent is not None:
        specs = (
            (512, False, 0.25),
            (1024, True, 0.60),
            (2048, True, 0.15),
        )

    if total >= 1000.0 and parent is None:
        specs = (
            (1024, True, 0.18),
            (2048, True, 0.48),
            (4096, True, 0.26),
            (8192, True, 0.08),
        )
    elif total >= 700.0 and parent is None:
        specs = (
            (1024, True, 0.20),
            (2048, True, 0.50),
            (4096, True, 0.30),
        )
    elif total >= 300.0 and parent is None:
        specs = (
            (1024, True, 0.34),
            (2048, True, 0.56),
            (4096, True, 0.10),
        )
    else:
        specs = (
            (1024, True, 0.70),
            (2048, True, 0.30),
        )

    cumulative = 0.0
    for n, sym, frac in specs:
        n = int(n)
        end = t0 + main_budget * (cumulative + float(frac))
        cumulative += float(frac)
        end = min(float(end), main_deadline - 2.0)
        if end <= time.time() + 15.0:
            continue

        start_full = warm_full
        if start_full is None:
            start_full = best_full
        if start_full is None or start_full.size == 0:
            start_full = resample_full(np.full(64, 0.5), n)

        if sym:
            tmp = resample_full(start_full, n)
            x0 = half_from_full(tmp, n)
        else:
            x0 = resample_full(start_full, n)
            x0 = project_box_sum(x0, 0.5 * float(n))

        x1, _ = optimize_vars(x0, n, sym, end)
        x1, h1, c1 = finalize_vars(x1, n, sym)
        if not np.isfinite(c1):
            continue
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_vars = x1
            best_full = h1
            best_sym = bool(sym)
            best_n = n
            best_c = float(c1)
        if c1 <= best_c + max(1e-7, 1e-4 * max(1.0, best_c)):
            warm_full = h1

    # Final polish on the currently best exact solution.
    if best_vars is not None and time.time() < final_deadline - 12.0:
        nb = int(best_n)
        sym_b = bool(best_sym)
        target_b = target_for(nb, sym_b)
        nfft = next_pow2(2 * nb - 1)
        ones = np.zeros(nfft, dtype=float)
        ones[:nb] = 1.0
        onesfft = np.fft.rfft(ones)

        if time.time() < final_deadline - 20.0:
            sub_end = min(final_deadline - 10.0, time.time() + max(10.0, 0.35 * (final_deadline - time.time())))
            if sub_end > time.time() + 4.0:
                x2, _ = subgrad_refine(best_vars, nb, sym_b, sub_end, nfft, onesfft, target_b)
                x2, h2, c2 = finalize_vars(x2, nb, sym_b)
                if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                    best_vars = x2
                    best_full = h2
                    best_sym = sym_b
                    best_n = nb
                    best_c = float(c2)
                if c2 <= best_c + max(1e-7, 1e-4 * max(1.0, best_c)):
                    warm_full = h2

        if time.time() < final_deadline - 4.0:
            x2, _ = pair_local_search(best_vars, nb, sym_b, final_deadline - 1.0, nfft, onesfft, target_b)
            x2, h2, c2 = finalize_vars(x2, nb, sym_b)
            if np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c:
                best_vars = x2
                best_full = h2
                best_sym = sym_b
                best_n = nb
                best_c = float(c2)

    if best_vars is None:
        nb = 1024
        x2 = default_vars(nb, True, seed, 1)
        best_vars, best_full, best_c = finalize_vars(x2, nb, True)
        best_sym = True
        best_n = nb

    best_vars, best_full, best_c = finalize_vars(best_vars, int(best_n), bool(best_sym))
    if not np.isfinite(best_c) or best_full is None or best_full.size == 0:
        nb = 1024
        x2 = default_vars(nb, True, seed, 1)
        best_vars, best_full, best_c = finalize_vars(x2, nb, True)
        best_sym = True
        best_n = nb

    return best_full, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
